description|Professional Fighters League MMA event in 2023}}\n\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=PFL Europe 1\n|image=PFL Europe 1.jpg\n|image_size=\n|promotion=[[Professional Fighters 10]]\n|followingevent=[[PFL 1 (2023 season)|PFL 1]]\n}}\n{{Main|PFL (2023 season)}}\n\nThe '''''PFL Europe 1''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2023 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] Europe was held on March 25, 2023, at the [[Vertu Motors Arena]] in [[Newcastle upon Tyne|Newcastle]], England. This was the inaugural event of the PFL Europe league, holding bouts in the [[Light Heavyweight (MMA)|Light Heavyweight]] and Women's [[Flyweight (MMA)|Flyweight]]{{cite web |last1=King |first1=Nolan |title=Inaugural PFL Europe season schedule announced |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/01/mma-news-pfl-europe-season-launch-2023 |website=MMA Junkie |access-date=21 February 2023 |date=12 January 2023}}{{cite web |last1=Anderson |first1=Jay |title=PFL Europe Announces Full 2023 Roster, Includes Four Weight Classes |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/02/14/pfl-europe-announces-full-2023-roster-includes-four-weight-classes/ |website=Cageside Press |access-date=21 February 2023 |date=14 February 2023}}\n\n== Background ==\nStarting in 2023 PFL Europe will feature young European MMA fighters, broadcast during prime local hours with all events staged in Europe. The format will follow the same as the regular PFL Season, with the winner receiving a $100,000 prize and a chance to earn a spot in the 2024 PFL regular season.\n\nAt weigh-ins, Mafalda Carmona, who was supposed to fight Griet Eeckhout in the flyweight category, weighed in at 131.2 pounds, which was 5.2 pounds over the limit. Consequently, the fight was called off. Lewis Monarch weighed in at 157 pounds for his lightweight showcase bout against Ownsworth, exceeding the 156-pound limit by one pound. However, the bout went ahead with Monarch being penalized a certain percentage of his earnings.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Europe 1 Weigh-in Results: 2 Fighters Heavy, 1 Fight Nixed This was the finale of the playoffs for all divisions, with the winners getting $1 million cash prize.{{Cite web|last=Sherdog.com|title=PFL Sets 2021 Season Finale For October, Announces Claressa Shields' Return|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Sets-2021-Season-Finale-For-October-Announces-Claressa-Shields-Return-181054|access-date=2021-08-12|website=Sherdog}}{{Cite web|last=Guillen|first=Adam Jr.|date=2021-08-12|title=Shields Booked For PFL Championship Event On Oct. 27|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2021/8/12/22621700/claressa-shields-booked-second-fight-abigail-montes-pfl-championship-event-abigail-montes-mma|access-date=2021-08-12|website=MMAmania.com|language=en}}{{Cite web|last=Heck|first=Mike|date=2021-08-12|title=Claressa Shields vs. Abigail Montes set for PFL championship event on Oct. 27|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/12/22621546/claressa-shields-vs-abigail-montes-set-for-pfl-championship-event-on-oct-27|access-date=2021-08-12|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\n==Background==\n[[Kayla Harrison]], a two-time Olympic gold medalist in judo and is seeking her second PFL championship, headlined the PFL event opposite Taylor Guardado, who emerged as an unexpected finalist in 2021. The event also featured five other finals matchups, with the winner of each weight class taking home PFL's trademark $1 million prize.{{Cite web|last=Alexander|first=Mookie|date=2021-08-28|title=Here are the six tournament finals for the PFL 2021 season finale|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/8/28/22646143/mma-news-six-tournament-finals-pfl-2021-season-finale|access-date=2021-09-27|website=Bloody Elbow|language=en}}\n\nThe five other championship bouts included a welterweight rematch between [[Ray Cooper III]] and [[Magomed Magomedkerimov]], [[Movlid Khaybulaev]] versus UFC vet [[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]] at featherweight, a heavyweight rematch between Ante Delija and [[Bruno Cappelozza]], UFC vet [[Ant\u00f4nio Carlos J\u00fanior]] taking on Marthin Hamlet at light heavyweight, and Loik Radzhabov rematching [[Raush Manfio]] at lightweight.{{Cite web|date=2021-09-27|title=Undefeated Harrison headlines PFL's 2021 Finals|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/32292101/undefeated-lightweight-kayla-harrison-headlines-pfl-2021-finals|access-date=2021-09-27|website=ESPN.com|language=en}}\n\nThis PFL event featured the second MMA appearance of boxing champion [[Claressa Shields]]. Shields, who won her professional MMA debut in June, took on Mexico's Abigail Montes.{{Cite web|last=Sherdog.com|title=Full Lineup Announced for Professional Fighters League Championships on Oct. 27|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Full-Lineup-Announced-for-Professional-Fighters-League-Championships-on-Oct-27-181946|access-date=2021-09-27|website=Sherdog}}\n\nFormer UFC middleweight [[Omari Akhmedov]] made a non-tournament appearance on the card. her third PFL championship, headlined the PFL event opposite [[Larissa Pacheco]], who previously faced Harrison in two other title bouts, losing both by unanimous decision. The event also featured five other finals matchups, with the winner of each weight class taking home PFL's trademark $1 million dollar prize.\n\nThe five other championship bouts included a welterweight bout between [[Sadibou Sy]] and Dilano Taylor, [[Brendan Loughnane]] versus Bellator vet [[Bubba Jenkins]] at featherweight, a heavyweight bout between Ante Delija and Matheus Scheffel, UFC vets [[Rob Wilkinson]] and [[Omari Akhmedov]] at light heavyweight, and another pair of UFC vets in [[Stevie Ray (fighter)|Stevie Ray]] and [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] at lightweight.\n\nThe event was to feature the PFL debuts of [[Shane Burgos]] and former [[List of Professional Fighters League champions#Bantamweight Championship|WSOF Bantamweight Champion]] [[Marlon Moraes]], facing off in a featherweight bout. However, Burgos withdrew from the bout due to an injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/11/shane-burgos-injured-withdraws-from-pfl-debut-fight-vs-marlon-moraes|title=Shane Burgos (30-27, 30-27, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n==Standings After Event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web|url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|title=Rules and Regulations|publisher=PFL|access-date=2019-04-28|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|archive-date=2018-08-08|url-status=dead}}\n\n===Welterweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! This was the first regular season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[featherweight (MMA)|Featherweight]] and [[lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] divisions.{{cite web|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2021-pfl-1|title=Fight Card - 2021 PFL 1|publisher=PFL}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a lightweight clash between the former [[UFC]] and [[World Extreme Cagefighting|WEC]] Lightweight champion [[Anthony Pettis]] and the [[UFC]] veteran [[Clay Collard]]. In the co-main event, the two-time PFL Lightweight tournament winner [[Natan Schulte]] faced the former [[Bellator MMA|Bellator]] Lightweight title challenger [[Marcin Held]].{{cite web |last1=Head |first1=Simon |title=Anthony Pettis vs. Clay Collard to headline PFL 2021 season opener |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/pfl-2021-week-1-anthony-pettis-vs-clay-collard-headline-season-opener |website=mmajunkie.usatoday.com |access-date=23 February 2021}}\n\nThe remaining two fights of the main card were held in the featherweight division: former two-time PFL Featherweight champion [[Lance Palmer]] faced the 2011 [[NCAA Division I Wrestling Championships|NCAA Division I champion]] [[Bubba Jenkins]] and [[Movlid Khaybulaev]] was to face Jason Soares.{{cite web |last1=Sukhnev |first1=Igor |title=\u041e\u0431\u044a\u044f\u0432\u043b\u0435\u043d \u0441\u043e\u0441\u0442\u0430\u0432 1 \u043d\u0435\u0434\u0435\u043b\u0438 PFL 2021. \u0412 \u0433\u043b\u0430\u0432\u043d\u043e\u043c \u0441\u043e\u0431\u044b\u0442\u0438\u0438 \u0434\u0435\u0431\u044e\u0442 \u042d\u043d\u0442\u043e\u043d\u0438 \u041f\u0435\u0442\u0442\u0438\u0441\u0430 |url=https://fight.ru/obyavlen-sostav-1-nedeli-pfl-2021-v-glavnom-sobytii-debyut-jentoni-pettisa/ |website=fight.ru |access-date=23 February 2021}} However, Soares was removed from the PFL season and his fight against Movlid Khaibulaev after failing medicals.{{cite web |last1=Pettry |first1=Jay |title=Curtis Millender Joins PFL 2021 Tournament, Faces Rory MacDonald on April 29 |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Curtis-Millender-Joins-PFL-2021-Tournament-Faces-Rory-MacDonald-on-April-29-178679 |website=Sherdog |access-date=9 April 2021}} He was replaced by Lazar Stojadinovic.\n\nA lightweight bout between Joilton Lutterbach and [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] was planned for this event, however Olivier pulled out due to injury and was replaced by Former [[Titan FC]] lightweight champion [[Raush Manfio]]. Olivier is expected to return in June.{{cite web |last1=Cruz |first1=Guilherme |title=Raush Manfio replaces injured Olivier Aubin-Mercier against Joilton Lutterbach at April 23 PFL card |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/23/22347181/raush-manfio-replaces-injured-olivier-aubin-mercier-joilton-lutterbach-pfl |website=MMA Fighting |access-date=23 March 2021 |language=en |date=23 March 2021}}\n\n[[Johnny Case]] was expected to face Loik Radzhabov at this event.{{cite web |last1=Head |first1=Simon |title=Anthony Pettis vs. Clay Collard to headline PFL 2021 season opener |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/02/pfl-2021-week-1-anthony-pettis-vs-clay-collard-headline-season-opener |website=mmajunkie.usatoday.com |access-date=23 February 2021}} However, due to an arrest, Case had to pull out and was replaced by Alexander Martinez.{{cite tweet |author=PFL |user=PFLMMA |number=1379486465877544962 |date=6 April 2021 |title=ROSTER UPDATE: @Mmartinez_alex steps in for Johnny Case at #PFL1 PFL 1 Friday, April 23 5:30P ET ESPN+ {{!}} 9P ET ESPN2 https://t.co/eE4X9aSDu8 |language=en |access-date=14 June 2021}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, Mikhail Odintsov weighed in at 156.4 pounds, missing weight by .4 pounds. He was fined 20 percent of his purse, ineligible to win playoff points, given a walkover loss, and was penalized one point in the standings. Akhmet Aliev received a walkover win regardless of bout outcome but is eligible to gain stoppage points.{{cite web |title=PFL 1: 2021 Regular Season Weigh-in Results: Pettis vs. Collard Set; Mikhail Odintsov Heavy |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-1-2021-Regular-Season-Weighin-Results-Pettis-vs-Collard-Set-Mikhail-Odintsov-Heavy-178884 |website=Sherdog |access-date=22 April 2021}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN 2)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Clay Collard]]\n|def.\n|[[Anthony Pettis]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29-27, 29-27, 29-28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Marcin Held]]\n|def.\n|[[Natan Schulte]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29-28, 29-28, 29-28)\n|3\n|5:00\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Movlid Khaybulaev]]\n|def.\n|Lazar Stojadinovic\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 30-27, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Bubba Jenkins]]\n|def.\n|[[Lance Palmer]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 30-27, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Brendan Loughnane]]\n|def.\n|[[Sheymon Moraes]]\n|KO (punches)\n|1\n|2:55\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Raush Manfio]]\n|def.\n|Joilton Lutterbach\n|Decision (split) (28-29, 29-28, 29-28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|Akhmet Aliev\n|def.\n|Mikhail Odintsov\n|Decision (unanimous) (29-28, 29-28, 29-28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]]\n|def.\n|Anthony Dizy\n|Decision (unanimous) (29-28, 29-28, 29-28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|Tyler Diamond\n|def.\n|Sung Bin Jo\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 30-27, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|Alexander Martinez\n|def.\n|Loik Radzhabov\n|Decision (split) (29-28, 28-29, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n==Standings After Event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout, with the fighter who missed weight being deducted 1 standings point; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded. A fighter who was unable to compete for any reason, will receive a 1-point penalty (-1 point in the standings). The fighters who made weight will not receive a walkover, but will earn points and contracted purse amounts based on their performance in the altered matchup.{{Cite web|title=2021 Official Rules|url=https://pflmma-prod.s3.amazonaws.com/assets/downloads/2021_Rules_and_Regulations_4-26-2021.pdf}}\n\n===Lightweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! The remaining two fights of the main card saw defending light heavyweight champion [[Ant\u00f4nio Carlos J\u00fanior]] face off against Delan Monte, while 2019 champion [[Natan Schulte]] took on [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]].{{Cite web |date=2022-03-22 |title=Manfio vs. Madge to headline PFL season kickoff |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/33570255/raush-manfio-vs-don-magic-man-madge-headline-professional-fighters-league-2022-season-kickoff-former-ufc-champ-anthony-pettis-myles-price-co-main-event |access-date=2022-03-22 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}} \n\nA lightweight bout between former WEC and UFC Lightweight Champion [[Anthony Pettis]] and Bellator veteran Myles Price was scheduled for the event but the bout was later moved to [[PFL 3 (2022 season)|PFL 3]].{{Cite web |date=2022-04-05 |title=Kayla Harrison's first fight of 2022 headlines PFL 3; Rory MacDonald, Anthony Pettis on card |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/04/2022-pfl-3-kayla-harrison-headlines-rory-macdonald-anthony-pettis-return |access-date=2022-04-05 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN/ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Clay Collard]]\n|def.\n|[[Jeremy Stephens]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 30\u201327)\n|3\n|5:00\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Raush Manfio]]\n|def.\n|Don Madge\n|TKO (punches)\n|3\n|2:42\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Ant\u00f4nio Carlos J\u00fanior]]\n|def.\n|Delan Monte\n|Submission (brabo choke)\n|1\n|0:29\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]]\n|def.\n|[[Natan Schulte]]\n|Decision (split) (28\u201329, 29\u201328, 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|Cory Hendricks\n|def.\n|[[Emiliano Sordi]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|2\n|2:13\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|Alexander Martinez\n|def.\n|[[Stevie Ray (fighter)|Stevie Ray]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 29\u201328, 30\u201327)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|Marthin Hamlet\n|def.\n|Teodoras Auk\u0161tuolis\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 30-27, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Omari Akhmedov]]\n|def.\n|[[Viktor Pe\u0161ta]]\n|KO (punches)\n|1\n|1:25\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Rob Wilkinson]]\n|def.\n|Bruce Souto\n|TKO (punches)\n|2\n|0:46\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|Simeon Powell\n|def.\n|Clinton Williams\n|KO (knee)\n|3\n|4:38\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n== Standings After Event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Light Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! Fighters League]] was held on April 1, 2023, at [[The Theater at Virgin Hotels]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]], United States. This marked the first regular-season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Featherweight (MMA)|Featherweight]] and [[Light heavyweight (MMA)|Light Heavyweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Announces Headliners, Co-Main Events for First Three Regular Season Cards |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Announces-Headliners-CoMain-Events-for-First-Three-Regular-Season-Cards-189066 |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2023-02-02 |title=Loughnane-Moraes to kick off 2023 PFL season |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/35576696/brendan-loughnane-marlon-moraes-kick-2023-pfl-season |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=2023-02-02 |title=Brendan Loughnane vs. Marlon Moraes, Olivier Aubin-Mercier vs. Shane Burgos among fights announced for PFL in 2023 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/2/23582562/brendan-loughnane-vs-marlon-moraes-olivier-aubin-mercier-vs-shane-burgos-fights-booked-pfl-2023 |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe first event of 2023 feature a bout between [[Brendan Loughnane]] and [[Marlon Moraes]] in the featherweight division. Meanwhile, [[Rob Wilkinson]] faced [[Thiago Santos]], a former UFC title challenger, in the co-main event.\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Omari Akhmedov]] and [[Will Fleury]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web |date=2023-02-16 |title=2022 champs Brendan Loughnane, Rob Wilkinson headline 2023 PFL 1 on April 1 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/02/2023-pfl-1-las-vegas-mma-lineup-brendan-loughnane-rob-wilkinson |access-date=2023-02-20 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}} However, Akhmedov was forced to withdraw due to suffering an injury and was replaced by [[Krzysztof Jotko]].{{Cite web|url=https://severemma.com/2023/02/report-omari-akhmedov-out-krzystof-jotko-set-to-step-in-vs-will-fleury-in-1m-tournament/|title=Report: Omari Akhmedov Out; Krzystof Jotko set to step in vs. Will Fleury in $1m Tournament|website=SevereMMA.com - UFC, Mixed Martial Arts (MMA), Irish MMA|author=Ian O'Neill|date=2023-02-28|access-date=2023-03-01}}\n\nSung Bin Jo was expected to face [[Jesus Pinedo]]. However, Jo was not medically cleared and was replaced by alternate Gabriel Alves Braga.\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN / ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Brendan Loughnane]]\n|def.\n|[[Marlon Moraes]]\n|TKO (leg kicks)\n|2\n|1:11\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Rob Wilkinson]]\n|vs.\n|[[Thiago Santos]]\n|No Contest (overturned)\n|3\n|5:00\n|Originally a Decision (unanimous) win for Wilkinson; The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Light Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! Fighters League MMA event in 2019}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=PFL 2\n|image=File:PFL 2 (2019 season).jpg\n|promotion=[[Professional Fighters League]]\n|date= {{start date|2019|05|23}}\n|venue= (2019 season)}}\nThe '''''PFL 2''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2019 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on May 23, 2019, at the [[Nassau Coliseum]] in [[Uniondale, New York]]. This was the second regular season event of 2019 and included fights in the [[featherweight]] and [[lightweight]] divisions.{{cite web|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2019-pfl-2|title=Fight Card - 2019 PFL 2|publisher=PFL}}\n\n==Background==\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Alexandre Bezerra and Alexandre de Almeida both missed weight for their featherweight bouts. As a result, both fighters were pulled from the card and their respective opponents - Jeremy Kennedy and Luis Rafael Laurentino - faced each other on the preliminary card.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2019/05/pfl-2019-week-2-weigh-in-results|title=PFL 2019, Week 2 weigh-in results: One fighter pulled for 2019 season|publisher=MMA Junkie}}\n\n[[Ramsey Nijem]] was expected to face Ronys Torres at this event. However, Torres failed his medical tests prior to the weigh-ins and the bout was cancelled. Nijem was awarded three points toward a potential playoff spot.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Lance Palmer]]\n|def.\n|Alex Gilpin\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 30-27, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]]\n|def.\n|Nate Andrews\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 29-28, 29-28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|Andre Harrison\n|def.\n|Peter Petties\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 30-27, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|Akhmet Aliev\n|def.\n|Carlos Silva\n|KO (punches)\n|1\n|2:24\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|Islam Mamedov\n|def.\n|Ylies Djiroun\n|Decision (split) (29-28, 28-29, 29-28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Rashid Magomedov]]\n|def.\n|Loik Radzhabov\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 30-27, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (ESPN 2)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Natan Schulte]]\n|def.\n|Yincang Bao\n|Submission (rear-naked choke)\n|1\n|3:03\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|Luis Rafael Laurentino\n|def.\n|[[Jeremy Kennedy]]\n|TKO (head kick and punches)\n|1\n|0:23\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Movlid Khaybulaev]]\n|def.\n|[[Damon Jackson]]\n|KO (flying knee)\n|1\n|0:10\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Gadzhi Rabadanov]]\n|def.\n|[[Steven Siler]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30-27, 29-28, 30-27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n==Standings After Event==\nThe point system consists of outcome based scoring and bonuses for an early win. Under the outcome based scoring system, the winner of a fight receives 3 points and the loser receives 0 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. If a fighter misses weight, then the fighter that missed weight will receive 0 points and his opponent will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory.{{cite web|url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|title=Rules and Regulations|publisher=PFL|access-date=2019-04-29|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|archive-date=2018-08-08|url-status=dead}}\n\n===Featherweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! '''''PFL 2''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2021 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on April 29, 2021. This was the second regular season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] and [[light heavyweight (MMA)|Light heavyweight]] divisions.{{cite web|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2021-pfl-2|title=Fight Card - 2021 PFL 2|publisher=PFL}}\n\n==Background==\n \nThe event was to be headlined by a welterweight clash between the former UFC title contender and Bellator welterweight champion [[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] and the former UFC veteran [[David Michaud]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/2/22308795/rory-macdonald-faces-david-michaud-in-pfl-debut-as-welterweights-light-heavyweights-begin-april-29|title=Rory MacDonald faces David Michaud in PFL debut as welterweights, light heavyweights begin season on April 29|author=Damon Martin|work=MMAFighting.com|date=March 2, 2021|accessdate=March 2, 2021}} On April 8, Michaud announced that he had to pull out of the bout due to medicals discovering a congenital heart condition, congenital bicuspid aortic valve with dilated aortic root, which put him at risk of [[aortic dissection]]. His future in MMA is also uncertain.{{cite web |last1=Pettry |first1=Jay |title=Citing Heart Problems, David Michaud Withdraws From 2021 PFL Season |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Citing-Heart-Problems-David-Michaud-Withdraws-From-2021-PFL-Season-178673 |website=Sherdog |access-date=8 April 2021}} He was replaced for the season by [[Curtis Millender]].{{cite web |title=Curtis Millender steps in to fight Rory MacDonald at PFL 2021, Week 2 on April 29 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/pfl-2-curtis-millender-to-fight-rory-macdonald-replaces-david-michaud |website=MMA Junkie |access-date=9 April 2021 |date=9 April 2021}}\n\nIn the co-main event, the 2019 PFL Welterweight tournament winner [[Ray Cooper III]] is scheduled to fight Jason Monet.{{cite web |last1=Martin |first1=Damon |title=Rory MacDonald faces David Michaud in PFL debut as welterweights, light heavyweights begin season on April 29 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/2/22308795/rory-macdonald-faces-david-michaud-in-pfl-debut-as-welterweights-light-heavyweights-begin-april-29 |website=MMA Fighting |access-date=2 March 2021 |language=en |date=2 March 2021}}\n\nThe remaining two fights of the main card are scheduled in the light heavyweight division: [[Jordan Johnson (fighter)|Jordan Johnson]] will fight [[Tom Lawlor]], and 2019 PFL Light Heavyweight tournament winner [[Emiliano Sordi]] will fight former long time UFC fighter [[Chris Camozzi]].{{cite web |last1=Head |first1=Simon |title=PFL 2021, Week 2: Rory MacDonald set for debut as welterweight, light heavyweight seasons begin |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/03/pfl-2021-week-2-rory-macdonald-set-for-debut-as-welterweight-light-heavyweight-seasons-begin |website=MMA Junkie |access-date=2 March 2021 |date=2 March 2021}}\n\n[[Jordan Johnson (fighter)|Jordan Johnson]] was set to fight [[Tom Lawlor]] at this event. However, in March, it was announced that Johnson pulled out and was replaced by [[Ant\u00f4nio Carlos J\u00fanior]].{{cite web |last=Martin|first=Damon|title=Antonio Carlos Junior signs with PFL for Season 3, meets fellow UFC vet Tom Lawlor |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/7/22317190/antonio-carlos-junior-signs-with-pfl-for-season-3-meets-fellow-ufc-vet-tom-lawlor |website=MMA Fighting |access-date=8 March 2021 |language=en |date=7 March 2021}} Later, PFL announced that [[Ant\u00f4nio Carlos J\u00fanior]] will replace Johnson for the whole season.{{cite tweet |author=PFL |user=PFLMMA |number=1374481876329533441 |date=23 March 2021 |title=ROSTER UPDATES: \u2022 Antonio Carlos Jr. replaces Jordan Johnson for the 2021 Season \u2022 Raush Manfio steps in for Olivier Aubin-Mercier at #PFL1 https://t.co/IxV9NTtoHQ |language=en |access-date=14 June 2021}}\n\nOn March 25, it was announced that [[Magomed Magomedkerimov]] and [[Alexey Kunchenko]] pulled out of their bouts due to undisclosed reasons. Due to this, [[Gleison Tibau]] and Jo\u00e3o Zeferino were scheduled to face each other.{{Cite web|last=Sherdog.com|title=Gleison Tibau Meets Joao Zeferino at PFL 2 After Two Fighters Withdraw from Heavyweight\n|Marthin Hamlet\n|def.\n|[[Dan Spohn]]\n|Technical Submission (arm-triangle choke)\n|2\n|0:52\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n==Standings After Event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout, with the fighter who missed weight being deducted 1 standings point; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded. A fighter who was unable to compete for any reason, will receive a 1-point penalty (-1 point in the standings). The fighters who made weight will not receive a walkover, but will earn points and contracted purse amounts based on their performance in the altered matchup.{{Cite web|title=2021 Official Rules|url=https://pflmma-prod.s3.amazonaws.com/assets/downloads/2021_Rules_and_Regulations_4-26-2021.pdf}}\n\n===Light heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! |language=en-US}}\n\nThe remaining two fights of the main card saw heavyweight finalist Ante Delija face off against Matheus Scheffel, while the [[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]] faced off against Jamelle Jones.{{Cite web |title=Brendan Loughnane, Lance Palmer among fighters set for 2022 PFL 2 card on April 28 |url=https://sports.yahoo.com/brendan-loughnane-lance-palmer-among-153042516.html |access-date=2022-03-29 |website=sports.yahoo.com |language=en-US}}\n\nBubba Jenkins was scheduled to face Sung Bin Jo at this event.{{Cite web |date=2022-03-29 |title=Cappelozza, Austin to headline PFL 2 on April 28 Sungbin pulled out of the bout, he was replaced by [[Kyle Bochniak]].{{Cite web |date=2022-04-14 |title=Kyle Bochniak signs with PFL, steps in to face Bubba Jenkins at 2022 PFL 2 (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n== Standings After Event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! Fighters League]] was held on April 7, 2023, at [[The Theater at Virgin Hotels]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], United States. This marked the second regular-season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] and Women's [[Featherweight (MMA)|Featherweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Announces Headliners, Co-Main Events for First Three Regular Season Cards |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Announces-Headliners-CoMain-Events-for-First-Three-Regular-Season-Cards-189066 |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2023-02-02 |title=Loughnane-Moraes to kick off 2023 PFL season |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/35576696/brendan-loughnane-marlon-moraes-kick-2023-pfl-season |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=2023-02-02 |title=Brendan Loughnane vs. Marlon Moraes, Olivier Aubin-Mercier vs. Shane Burgos among fights announced for PFL in 2023 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/2/23582562/brendan-loughnane-vs-marlon-moraes-olivier-aubin-mercier-vs-shane-burgos-fights-booked-pfl-2023 |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\n== Background ==\n[[Ante Delija]] was to headline the event in a bout against [[Yorgan De Castro]] in a heavyweight match-up. However, Delija pulled out due to injury and was replaced by Denis Goltsov.{{Cite web |last=Cruz |first=Guilherme |date=2023-03-22 |title=PFL heavyweight champion Ante Delija injured, out of Yorgan de Castro fight on April 7 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/3/22/23652047/2022-pfl-heavyweight-champion-ante-delija-injured-out-yorgan-de-castro-fight |access-date=2023-03-25 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}} In turn, Goltsov withdrew from the bout and was replaced by [[Danilo Marques]].{{Cite web |last=Subramanian |first=Anik |date=2023-04-06 |title=Despite multiple opponent changes, Yorgan De Castro will bring his smile to the smart cage tomorrow |url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/despite-multiple-opponent-changes-yorgan-de-castro-will-bring-his-smile-to-the-smart-cage-tomorrow/ |access-date=2023-04-10 |website=FightBook MMA |language=en-US}}\n\nIn the new women's featherweight division, [[Larissa Pacheco]], who caused an upset in the 2022 finals by defeating two-time women's lightweight champion [[Kayla Harrison]], will take on former [[Bellator MMA]] champion [[Julia Budd]].\n\nAt weigh-ins, Kolesnyk came in at 146.4 lbs, .4 pounds over the limit for featherweight. She was fined 20% of her purse and was given a point deduction in the standings.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL 2 2023 Regular Season Weigh-in Results: 1 Fighter Heavy |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-2-2023-Regular-Season-Weighin-Results-1-Fighter-Heavy-189618 |access-date=2023-04-08 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN2 / ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Featherweight\n|[[Larissa Pacheco]]\n|def.\n|[[Julia Budd]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 29\u201328, 30\u201327)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Bruno Cappelozza]]\n|vs.\n|Matheus Scheffel\n|No Contest (overturned)\n|1\n|2:15\n|Originally a TKO (punches) win for Cappelozza; The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! The bout was held as scheduled. Heavyweight\n|[[Bazigit Atajev]]\n|def.\n|[[Dan Spohn]]\n|KO (punches)\n|1\n|3:25\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n==Standings After Event==\nThe point system consists of outcome based scoring and bonuses for an early win. Under the outcome based scoring system, the winner of a fight receives 3 points and the loser receives 0 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. If a fighter misses weight, then the fighter that missed weight will receive 0 points and his opponent will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory.{{cite web|url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|title=Rules and Regulations|publisher=PFL|access-date=2019-04-29|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|archive-date=2018-08-08|url-status=dead}}\n\n===Light Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! This was the third regular season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] and women's [[lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] divisions.{{cite web|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2021-pfl-3|title=Fight Card - 2021 PFL 3|publisher=PFL}} After the previous two events aired on [[ESPN2]], this was the first event airing on [[ESPN]].{{Cite web|date=2021-05-03|title=Ali Isaev, Hatef Moeil ruled out at 2021 PFL 3; However, Willis was pulled from the bout after the weigh-ins due to being not medically cleared and replaced by Muhammed DeReese.{{Cite web|date=2021-05-06|title=2021 PFL 3 weigh-in results: Fabricio Werdum official for PFL debut; Muhammed DeReese replaces Justin Willis|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/2021-pfl-3-weigh-in-results-fabricio-werdum-pfl-debut-muhammad-dereese-replaces-justin-willis|access-date=2021-05-06|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN)}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Heavyweight|[[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]]|vs.|[[Fabr\u00edcio Werdum]]|No Contest (overturned)|1|2:32|Originally an TKO (punches) win for Ferreira; later ruled a no contest after it was determined Ferreira tapped out to a triangle choke applied by Werdum. Due to this decision, both Werdum and Ferreria received one point in the standings.{{Cite web|last=Marrocco|first=Steven|date=2021-05-10|title=New Jersey commission overturns Renan Ferreira's PFL 3 win over Fabricio Werdum|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/10/22429538/new-jersey-commission-overturns-renan-ferreiras-pfl-3-win-over-fabricio-werdum|access-date=2021-05-10|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\n==Standings After Event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, no contest, or no decision, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout, with the fighter who missed weight being deducted 1 standings point; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded. A fighter who was unable to compete for any reason, will receive a 1-point penalty (-1 point in the standings). The fighters who made weight will not receive a walkover, but will earn points and contracted purse amounts based on their performance in the altered matchup.{{Cite web|title=2021 Official Rules|url=https://pflmma-prod.s3.amazonaws.com/assets/downloads/2021_Rules_and_Regulations_4-26-2021.pdf}}\n\n===Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! martial arts]] event for the 2022 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on May 6, 2022, at the [[Esports Stadium Arlington]] in [[Arlington, Texas]]. It was the third regular season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Lightweight (MMA)|Women's Lightweight]] and [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |date=2022-04-05 |title=Harrison, Cooper to headline PFL3 event |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/33674987/two-women-lightweight-champ-kayla-harrison-men-welterweight-champ-ray-cooper-iii-headline-professional-fighters-league-2022-regular-season-opener |access-date=2022-04-05 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2022-04-05 |title=Kayla Harrison's first fight of 2022 headlines PFL 3; Rory MacDonald, Anthony Pettis on card |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/04/2022-pfl-3-kayla-harrison-headlines-rory-macdonald-anthony-pettis-return |access-date=2022-04-05 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=Defending Champs Kayla Harrison, Ray Cooper III Highlight PFL Week 3 Lineup |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Defending-Champs-Kayla-Harrison-Ray-Cooper-III-Highlight-PFL-Week-3-Lineup-185798 |access-date=2022-04-05 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe event will be headlined by a women's lightweight clash between the 2019 and 2021 champion [[Kayla Harrison]] and Bellator vet [[Marina Mokhnatkina]]. The co-main will feature a welterweight bout between 2019 and 2021 champion [[Ray Cooper III]] facing undefeated [[Magomed Umalatov]].{{Cite web |last=Newswire |first=MMA Fighting |date=2022-04-05 |title=Kayla Harrison, Anthony Pettis and Rory MacDonald headline PFL 3 card on May 6 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/4/5/23010968/kayla-harrison-anthony-pettis-and-rory-macdonald-headline-pfl-3-card-on-may-6 |access-date=2022-04-05 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}} [[Magomed Umalatov|Umalatov]] would pull out of the bout and be replaced by former [[Legacy Fighting Alliance#LFA Welterweight Championship|LFA Welterweight Champion]] Carlos Leal.{{Cite web |date=2022-04-30 |title=\u041c\u0430\u0433\u043e\u043c\u0435\u0434 \u0423\u043c\u0430\u043b\u0430\u0442\u043e\u0432 \u0432\u044b\u0431\u044b\u0432\u0430\u0435\u0442 \u0441 \u043f\u043e\u0435\u0434\u0438\u043d\u043a\u0430 \u043f\u0440\u043e\u0442\u0438\u0432 \u0447\u0435\u043c\u043f\u0438\u043e\u043d\u0430 PFL |url=https://mma.metaratings.ru/news/magomed-umalatov-vybyvaet-s-poedinka-protiv-chempiona-pfl-1256810/ |access-date=2022-05-02 |website=mma.metaratings.ru |language=ru}}\n\nThe remaining two fights of the main card will see lightweight bout between ex-UFC champ [[Anthony Pettis]] facing Bellator veteran Myles Price, while former Bellator Welterweight champion [[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] faces off against Bellator and ACA vet [[Brett Cooper (fighter)|Brett Cooper]].\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Magomed Magomedkerimov]] and Jo\u00e3o Zeferino was scheduled for this event, however Magomedkerimov pulled out of the bout due to visa issues and was replaced by Dilano Taylor.{{Cite web |title=Magomed Magomedkerimov is out of tomorrow\u2019s 2022 PFL 3 due to visa issues |url=https://twitter.com/mma_kings/status/1522217103817728001 |access-date=2022-05-05 |website=Twitter |language=en}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, [[Ray Cooper III]] and [[Genah Fabian]] missed weight for their bouts. Cooper weighed in at 176.4 pounds, 5.4 pounds over the welterweight non-title fight limit. Fabian weighed in at 160.8 pounds, 4.8 pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. They were fined 20 percent of their purses, ineligible to win playoff points, given a walkover loss, and were penalized one point in the standings. Carlos Leal and [[Julia Budd]] received walkover wins regardless of bout outcome but are eligible to gain stoppage points.{{Cite web |date=2022-05-05 |title=2022 PFL 3 weigh-in results: Two fighters, including two-time champ, come in heavy |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/2022-pfl-3-weigh-in-results-2021-champion-ray-cooper-iii-genah-fabian-miss-weight |access-date=2022-05-05 30\u201327)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n== Standings After Event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Welterweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! Fighters League]] was held on April 14, 2023, at [[The Theater at Virgin Hotels]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]], United States. This marked the third regular-season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] and [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=PFL Announces Headliners, Co-Main Events for First Three Regular Season Cards |url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-Announces-Headliners-CoMain-Events-for-First-Three-Regular-Season-Cards-189066 |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2023-02-02 |title=Loughnane-Moraes to kick off 2023 PFL season |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/35576696/brendan-loughnane-marlon-moraes-kick-2023-pfl-season |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Martin |first=Damon |date=2023-02-02 |title=Brendan Loughnane vs. Marlon Moraes, Olivier Aubin-Mercier vs. Shane Burgos among fights announced for PFL in 2023 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/2/2/23582562/brendan-loughnane-vs-marlon-moraes-olivier-aubin-mercier-vs-shane-burgos-fights-booked-pfl-2023 |access-date=2023-02-02 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe first round of the PFL regular season ended at this event, with [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] facing [[Shane Burgos]], a free-agent acquisition in the lightweight division. In the welterweight division, [[Sadibou Sy]] met Jarrah Al-Silawi.\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Lightweight|[[Natan Schulte]]|def.|[[Stevie Ray (fighter)|Stevie Ray]]|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 30\u201327)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Lightweight|[[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]]|def.|[[Shane Burgos]]|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 30\u201327)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Lightweight|[[Clay Collard]]|def.|Yamato Nishikawa|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 30\u201327)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Heavyweight|[[Denis Goltsov]]|def.|[[Cezar Ferreira]]|KO (punches)|1|2:07|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|Carlos Leal|def.|[[David Zawada]]|TKO (punches)|1|2:25|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|[[Magomed Umalatov]]|def.|Dilano Taylor|KO (punches)|1|1:31|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Postliminary card (ESPN2 / ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|[[Sadibou Sy]]|def.|Jarrah Al-Silawi|TKO (knee to the body and punches)|2|1:14|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|[[Magomed Magomedkerimov]]|def.|Ben Egli|TKO (head kick)|1|1:03|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Lightweight|[[Raush Manfio]]|def.|Alexander Martinez|Decision (unanimous) (29\u201328, 29\u201328, 30\u201327)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|Nayib Lopez|def.|Shane Mitchell|Decision (unanimous) (29\u201327, 29\u201327, 29\u201327)|3|5:00|Lopez deducted one point for an illegal knee in round one.\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Lightweight|Bruno Miranda|def.|Ahmed Amir|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 30\u201327)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|Zach Juusola|def.|Brandon Jenkins|Decision (unanimous) (29\u201328, 29\u201328, 29\u201327)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web |title=PFL 3|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2023-pfl-3|website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=2 February 2023}}\n\n== Standings after event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! This was the fourth regular season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[featherweight (MMA)|Featherweight]] and [[lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] divisions.{{Cite web|date=2021-05-13|title=Anthony Pettis to headline 2021 PFL 4 as lightweights, featherweights get second fights|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/pfl-4-anthony-pettis-headlines-claress-shields-makes-debut|access-date=2021-05-14|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}{{Cite web|title=PFL 4|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2021-pfl-4|access-date=2021-05-25|website=www.pflmma.com}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was to be headlined by a lightweight clash between the former [[UFC]] and [[World Extreme Cagefighting|WEC]] Lightweight champion [[Anthony Pettis]] and undefeated Alexander Martinez. However, Pettis withdrew due to illness and Martinez instead faced [[Natan Schulte]]. Schulte's opponent [[Raush Manfio]] - who replaced an injured Mikhail Odintsov- will now face Pettis at [[PFL 6 (2021 season)|PFL 6]] on June 25, 2021.{{Cite web|title=Anthony Pettis passa mal e \u00e9 retirado de card do PFL 4; Natan Schulte o substitui|url=https://www.uol.com.br/esporte/ultimas-noticias/ag-fight/2021/06/09/anthony-pettis-passa-mal-e-e-retirado-de-card-do-pfl-4-natan-schulte-o-substitui.htm|access-date=2021-06-09|website=www.uol.com.br|language=pt-br}}\n\nIn the co-main event, the 2011 [[NCAA Division I Wrestling Championships|NCAA Division I champion]] [[Bubba Jenkins]] was to face Anthony Dizy.{{Cite web|last=Holland|first=Jesse|date=2021-05-13|title=Anthony Pettis vs Alex Martinez booked for PFL 4 on June 10|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2021/5/13/22434804/anthony-pettis-vs-alex-martinez-booked-pfl-4-june-10-mma|access-date=2021-05-14|website=MMAmania.com|language=en}} However, Dizy withdrew and Jenkins instead faced [[Bobby Moffett]].\n\nBoxing champion and Olympic gold medalist [[Claressa Shields]] made MMA debut on the main card of this event against Brittney Elkin.{{cite web |last1=Martin |first1=Damon |title=Boxing champ Claressa Shields faces Brittney Elkin in MMA debut as part of PFL card on June 10 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/14/22383678/boxing-champ-claressa-shields-faces-brittney-elkin-in-mma-debut-as-part-of-pfl-card-on-june-10 |website=MMA Fighting |access-date=14 April 2021 |language=en |date=14 April 2021}}\n\n[[Dana White's Contender Series]] alumni [[Brendan Loughnane]] faced [[The Ultimate Fighter 27]] contestant Tyler Diamond to round out the main card.{{Cite web|last=Lee|first=Alexander K.|date=2021-05-13|title=PFL announces full card for June 10 featuring Anthony Pettis vs. Alexander Martinez, Claressa Shields debut|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/13/22434759/pfl-announces-full-card-for-june-10-featuring-anthony-pettis-vs-alex-martinez-claressa-shields-debut|access-date=2021-05-20|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\nLazar Stojadinovic was supposed to face [[Lance Palmer]], but it was announced he would not compete at PFL 4. Palmer was then scheduled to face Jesse Stirn.{{Cite web|last=Heck|first=Mike|date=2021-05-25|title=Lance Palmer, Bubba Jenkins get new opponents for PFL 4|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/25/22453023/lance-palmer-bubba-jenkins-get-new-opponents-for-pfl-4|access-date=2021-05-26|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}} However, Palmer and Movlid Khaybulaev were later scratched from their bouts and rebooked to face each other at [[PFL 6 (2021 season)|PFL 6]]. Their original opponents, Jesse Stirn and [[Sheymon Moraes]], faced each other at this event instead.{{Cite web|last=Martin|first=Damon|date=2021-06-09|title=PFL 4 card loses two-time champ Lance Palmer, rescheduled with new opponent at PFL 6|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/9/22526492/pfl-4-card-loses-two-time-champ-lance-palmer-rescheduled-with-new-opponent-at-pfl-6|access-date=2021-06-09|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, Joilton Lutterbach and Jesse Stirn weighed in at 159.4 and 146.8 pounds, missing weight by 3.4 and .8 pounds respectively. They were fined 30 and 20 percent respectively of their purses, deemed ineligible to win playoff points, given a walkover loss, and were penalized one point in the standings. (fighter)|Chris Wade]]\n|def.\n|Arman Ospanov\n|KO (head kick and punches)\n|2\n|2:18\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n==Standings After Event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web|url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|title=Rules and Regulations|publisher=PFL|access-date=2019-04-28|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|archive-date=2018-08-08|url-status=dead}}\n\n===Lightweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! Event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Light Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! This marked the fourth regular-season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Featherweight]] and [[Light heavyweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |last=Sherdog.com |title=Professional Fighters League \u2013 PFL 4: 2023 Regular Season |url=https://www.sherdog.com/events/Professional-Fighters-League-PFL-4-2023-Regular-Season-96899 |access-date=2023-04-17 |website=Sherdog |language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Fightmag |title=PFL 4 {{!}} Season 2023 {{!}} MMA Event |url=https://schedule.fightmag.com/show/pfl-4/ |access-date=2023-04-17 |website=FIGHTMAG |language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\nThe 2023 PFL regular season continued on June 8, featuring featherweight and light heavyweight matches. After the first three events in Las Vegas, the series moves to Overtime Elite Arena in Atlanta, with live broadcasts on ESPN and ESPN+.{{Cite web |date=2023-04-26 |title=2023 PFL 4 lineup set: Brendan Loughnane, Robert Wilkinson, Thiago Santos return to action June 8 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2023/04/2023-pfl-4-full-fight-card-brendan-loughnane-robert-wilkinson-thiago-santos-return-june-8 |access-date=2023-04-27 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nIn the main event, 2022 featherweight champion [[Brendan Loughnane]] faced [[Jesus Pinedo]]. Loughnane started the 2023 season with a victory over [[Marlon Moraes]], while Pinedo lost a close match to Gabriel Braga. Loughnane leads the featherweight standings with 5 points.{{Cite web |date=2023-04-26 |title=PFL 4 : Brendan Loughnane vs Jesus Pinedo to headline card |url=https://bloodyelbow.com/2023/04/26/pfl-4-brendan-loughnane-vs-jesus-pinedo-to-headline-card/ |access-date=2023-04-27 |website=Bloody Elbow |language=en}}\n\nThe co-main event, in the light heavyweight division, was to have 2022 champion [[Rob Wilkinson]] going against Will Fleury. Wilkinson won his first match of the season against former UFC title challenger [[Thiago Santos]], while Fleury defeated [[Krzysztof Jotko]]. \n\nHowever, a bevy of failed drug tests changed this card as nine PFL fighters were suspended by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] included former UFC title challenger [[Thiago Santos]], former PFL heavyweight champion [[Bruno Cappelozza]], [[Krzysztof Jotko|Krzystof Jotko]], [[Rizvan Kuniev]], Mohammed Fakhreddine, [[Cezar Ferreira]], Will Fleury, Alejandro Flores, and Daniel Torres. In light of the recent suspensions, Ty Flores stepped in for Will Fleury to fight [[Rob Wilkinson]] in a light heavyweight match, but Wilkinson subsequently failed a drug test and was replaced by Dan Spohn;{{Cite web |date=2023-05-31 |title=PFL champ Rob Wilkinson pulled from 2023 season due to positive drug test \n|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/5/31/23743945/pfl-champ-rob-wilkinson-pulled-from-2023-season-due-to-positive-drug-test |access-date=2023-06-04 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}} Tyler Diamond replaced Daniel Torres against [[Movlid Khaybulaev]] in a featherweight bout; Gabriel Braga replaced Alejandro Flores to fight [[Marlon Moraes]] in another featherweight bout; Taylor Johnson and UFC veteran [[Andrew Sanchez]] joined the light heavyweight season to replace the suspended fighters and faced each other; [[Impa Kasanganay]] and Tim Caron, new additions to the light heavyweight season, competed against each other; and a scheduled feature fight between [[Thiago Santos]] and Mohammed Fakhreddine was cancelled.{{Cite web |date=2023-05-12 |title=Nine PFL fighters suspended, scrambling season |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/mma/story/_/id/37613472/nine-pfl-fighters-suspended-nevada-state-athletic-commission |access-date=2023-05-14 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, Ty Flores weighed in at 211.2 pounds, 5.2 pounds over the Light Heavyweight limit. He was fined 20% of his purse which went to his opponent Spohn and he was given a one point penalty in the standings and is also unable to win points in case he wins the bout.{{Cite web |last=Newswire |first=MMA Fighting |date=2023-06-07 |title=PFL 4 weigh-in video: Ty Flores misses weight by over 5 pounds |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/6/7/23752273/pfl-4-weigh-in-video-ty-flores-misses-weight-by-over-5-pounds |access-date=2023-06-08 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\n== Results |url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2023-pfl-4 |website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=17 June 2023}}\n\n== Standings after event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Light Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! This was the fifth regular season event of 2019 and include fights in the [[featherweight]] and [[lightweight]] divisions.{{cite web|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2019-pfl-5|title=Fight Card - 2019 PFL 5|publisher=PFL}}\n\n==Background==\nSeveral bouts were changed due to three fighters - lightweights [[Ramsey Nijem]] and Carlao Silva and featherweight Gadzhi Rabadanov - missing their weight marks for their respective bouts with all three removed from the card. As a result, defending lightweight champion [[Natan Schulte]], Nijem's original opponent, instead faced [[Jesse Ronson]], Silva's original opponent. [[Daniel Pineda (fighter)|Daniel Pineda]], Rabadanov's opponent, was awarded three points for a walkover victory.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (ESPN2)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]]\n|def.\n|Akhmet Aliev\n|Decision (unanimous) (29\u201328, 29\u201328, 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Lance Palmer]]\n|def.\n|Luis Rafael Laurentino\n|TKO (punches)\n|3\n|2:45\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|Nate Andrews\n|def.\n|[[Rashid Magomedov]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29\u201328, 29\u201328, 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|Alex Gilpin\n|def.\n|Freddy Assun\u00e7\u00e3o\n|Technical submission (guillotine choke)\n|1\n|2:08\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Jeremy Kennedy]]\n|def.\n|[[Steven Siler]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 29\u201328, 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Natan Schulte]]\n|def.\n|[[Jesse Ronson]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 30\u201327)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|Andre Harrison\n|vs.\n|[[Movlid Khaybulaev]]\n|Draw (majority) (29\u201328, 28\u201328, 28\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|Islam Mamedov\n|def.\n|Yincang Bao\n|TKO (punches)\n|1\n|3:32\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|Alexandre de Almeida\n|def.\n|Peter Petties\n|Decision (unanimous) (29\u201328, 29\u201328, 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|Loik Radzhabov\n|def.\n|Ylies Djiroun\n|Decision (split) (28-29, 29\u201328, 30\u201327)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n==Standings after event==\nThe point system consists of outcome based scoring and bonuses for an early win. Under the outcome based scoring system, the winner of a fight receives 3 points and the loser receives 0 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. If a fighter misses weight, then the fighter that missed weight will receive 0 points and his opponent will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory.{{cite web|url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|title=Rules and Regulations|publisher=PFL|access-date=2019-06-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/|archive-date=2018-08-08|url-status=dead}}\n\n===Featherweight===\n{{#section:2019 Professional Fighters League season|FTW}}\n\n===Lightweight===\n{{#section:2019 Professional Fighters League season|LW}}\n\u265b = Clinched playoff spot ---\nE = Eliminated\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of PFL events]]\n*[[List of current PFL fighters]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{World Series of Fighting Events}}\n{{2019 in mixed martial arts}}\n{{Authority control}}\n\n[[Category:Professional Fighters League events]]\n[[Category:2019 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts events in Atlantic City, New Jersey]]\n[[Category:2019 in sports in New Jersey]]\n[[Category:July 2019 sports events in the United States]]"}}}]},"67708793":{"pageid":67708793,"ns":0,"title":"PFL 5 (2021)","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","*":"{{Short description|Professional Fighters League mixed martial arts event in 2021}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=PFL 5\n|image=ProfessionalFighterLeague52021.jpg\n|image_size=250\n|promotion=[[Professional Fighters League]]\n|date= {{start date|2021|06|17}}\n|venue= [[Ocean Casino Resort]]\n|city= [[Atlantic City, New Jersey]], United States\n|attendance= \n|estimatedviewers = \n|previousevent=[[PFL 4 (2021 season)|PFL 4]]\n|followingevent=[[PFL 6 (2021 season)|PFL 6]]\n}}\n{{Main|PFL (2021 season)}}\nThe '''''PFL 5''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2021 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on June 17, 2021. This was the second regular season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] and [[light heavyweight (MMA)|Light heavyweight]] divisions.{{Cite web|title=PFL 5|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2021-pfl-5|access-date=2021-05-19|website=www.pflmma.com}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a welterweight clash between the former UFC title contender and Bellator welterweight champion [[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] and the former UFC veteran [[Gleison Tibau]].{{Cite web|agency=The Canadian Press|date=2021-05-19|title=Canadian welterweight Rory MacDonald to face Gleison Tibau at PFL 5 next month - TSN.ca|url=https://www.tsn.ca/canadian-welterweight-rory-macdonald-to-face-gleison-tibau-at-pfl-5-next-month-1.1642454|access-date=2021-05-20|website=TSN|language=en}}\n\nIn the co-main event, the 2019 PFL Welterweight tournament winner [[Ray Cooper III]] faced Nikolay Aleksakhin.{{Cite web|last=Newswire|first=MMA Fighting|date=2021-05-19|title=Rory MacDonald vs. Gleison Tibau headlines PFL 5 main card with welterweights and light heavyweights|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/19/22444075/rory-macdonald-vs-gleison-tibau-headlines-pfl-5-main-card-with-welterweights-and-light-heavyweights|access-date=2021-05-20|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\nThe remaining two fights of the main card featured [[Cezar Ferreira]] against [[Chris Camozzi]] in the Light Heavyweight division, and 2019 PFL Light Heavyweight tournament winner [[Emiliano Sordi]] facing former UFC and Bellator fighter [[Daniel Spohn]].{{Cite web|last=Anderson|first=Jay|date=2021-05-19|title=PFL 5 Topped by Rory MacDonald vs. Gleison Tibau on June 17|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/05/19/pfl-5-topped-rory-macdonald-vs-gleison-tibau-june-17/|access-date=2021-05-20|website=Cageside Press|language=en-US}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, [[Ray Cooper III]] weighed in at 171.8 pounds, missing weight by .8 pounds. He was fined 20 percent of his purse, deemed ineligible to win playoff points, given a walkover loss, and was penalized one point in the standings. Nikolay Aleksakhin received a walkover win regardless of bout outcome but is eligible to gain stoppage points.{{Cite web|last=Sherdog.com|title=PFL 5 Weigh-in Results: Main Event Set, 1 Fighter Heavy|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/PFL-5-Weighin-Results-Main-Event-Set-1-Fighter-Heavy-179963|access-date=2021-06-17|website=Sherdog}}{{Cite web|title=PFL 5, 2021: Weigh-in bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Sadibou Sy]]\n|def.\n|[[Alexey Kunchenko]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29-28, 29-28, 29-28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n==Standings After Event==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout, with the fighter who missed weight being deducted 1 standings point; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded. A fighter who was unable to compete for any reason, will receive a 1-point penalty (-1 point in the standings). The fighters who made weight will not receive a walkover, but will earn points and contracted purse amounts based on their performance in the altered matchup.{{Cite web|title=2021 Official Rules|url=https://pflmma-prod.s3.amazonaws.com/assets/downloads/2021_Rules_and_Regulations_4-26-2021.pdf}}\n\n===Light heavyweight===\n{{#section:2021 in Professional Fighters League|LHW}}\n\n===Welterweight===\n{{#section:2021 in Professional Fighters League season|WW}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of PFL events]]\n*[[List of current PFL fighters]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{World Series of Fighting Events}}\n{{2021 in mixed martial arts}}\n\n[[Category:Professional Fighters League events]]\n[[Category:2021 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:June 2021 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:Organized events in Atlantic City, New Jersey]]"}}}]},"70876177":{"pageid":70876177,"ns":0,"title":"PFL 5 (2022)","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","*":"{{short description|Professional Fighters League MMA event in 2022}}\n\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=PFL 5\n|image=File:PFL 5 (2022 season).jpg\n|promotion=[[Professional Fighters League]]\n|date={{start date|2022|06|24}}\n|venue=Overtime Elite Arena\n|city=[[Atlanta, Georgia]]\n|attendance=\n|estimatedviewers=\n|previousevent=[[PFL 4 (2022 season)|PFL 4]]\n|followingevent=[[PFL 6 (2022 season)|PFL 6]]\n}}\n{{Main|PFL (2022 season)}}\n\nThe '''''PFL 5''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2022 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on June 24, 2022 at the Overtime Elite Arena in [[Atlanta, Georgia]]. This marked the fifth regular season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] and [[Featherweight (MMA)|Featherweight]] divisions.\n\n== Background ==\nIn the main event, 2021 heavyweight champion [[Bruno Cappelozza]] faced Matheus Scheffel, with Cappelozza having his second headlining bout of the season following his first-round TKO victory [[PFL 2 (2022 season)|2022 PFL 2]]. The co-main event featured a lightweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Champion|UFC lightweight champion]] [[Anthony Pettis]] and fellow UFC veteran [[Stevie Ray (fighter)|Stevie Ray]]. The rest of the main card saw former UFC vets [[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]] and [[Kyle Bochniak]] facing off in a featherweight bout while heavyweights [[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]] and [[Klidson Abreu]] met in a second round bout.{{Cite web |date=2022-05-26 |title=2022 PFL 5 lineup set: Bruno Cappelozza headlines, Anthony Pettis vs. Stevie Ray in co-main The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points.
