Ago Huskic: $10,000\n\n== Aftermath ==\nOn September 23, it was announced that the Georgia Athletic and Entertainment Commission overturned the result of the bout between [[Klidson Abreu]] and [[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]] to a no contest, after Abreu failed a drug test for unspecified substances. Klidson and his team contended that the positive was due to prescribed antibiotics for a leg laceration.{{Cite web |last=Cruz |first=Guilherme |date=2022-09-23 |title=Klidson Abreu fails drug test, blames leg injury for now-overturned PFL 5 win over Renan Ferreira |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/9/23/23369027/klidson-abreu-fails-drug-test-pfl-5-win-renan-ferreira-overturned-no-contest-almost-lost-my-leg |access-date=2022-09-24 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\n== See also ==\n* [[List of PFL events]]\n* [[List of current PFL fighters]]\n\n== Notes ==\n{{Notelist}}\n\n== References ==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{World Series of Fighting Events}}\n{{2022 in mixed martial arts}}\n\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts events in Atlanta]]\n[[Category:Professional Fighters League events]]\n[[Category:2022 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:June 2022 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:2022 in sports in Georgia (U.S. state)]]\n[[Category:2022 in Atlanta]]"}}}]},"73795731":{"pageid":73795731,"ns":0,"title":"PFL 5 (2023)","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","*":"{{Short description|Mixed martial arts event}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n| name = PFL 5\n| image = PFL 5 2023 season.jpg\n| image_size = \n| promotion = [[Professional Fighters League]]\n| date = June 16, 2023\n| venue = Overtime Elite Arena\n| city = [[Atlanta]], Georgia, United States\n| attendance = \n| estimatedviewers = \n| previousevent = [[PFL 4 (2023 season)|PFL 4]]\n| followingevent = [[PFL 6 (2023 season)|PFL 6]]\n}}\n{{Main|2023 in Professional Fighters League}}\n\n'''''PFL 5''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Professional Fighters League]] that took place on June 16, 2023, at the Overtime Elite Arena in, [[Atlanta]], Georgia, United States. This marked the fifth regular-season event of the tournament and included fights in the [[Heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] and Women's [[Featherweight (MMA)|Featherweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |last=Lee |first=Alexander K. |date=2023-05-12 |title=PFL announces second-half event lineups following 9 fighters receiving drug test suspensions |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2023/5/12/23721590/pfl-announces-second-half-event-lineups-following-9-fighters-receiving-drug-test-suspensions |access-date=2023-05-14 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}{{Cite web |last=Anderson |first=Jay |date=2023-05-12 |title=Multiple Fighters Facing Suspension for Positive Drug Tests, PFL Announces Updated Fight Cards |url=https://cagesidepress.com/2023/05/12/multiple-fighters-facing-suspension-positive-drug-tests-pfl-announces-updated-s/ |access-date=2023-05-14 |website=Cageside Press |language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\n\nThis event was headlined by returning 2022 Heavyweight Champion Ante Delija facing UFC veteran [[Maurice Greene (fighter)|Maurice Greene]] and 2022 Women's Lightweight Champion [[Larissa Pacheco]] facing off against Bellator veteran [[Amber Leibrock]] in a featherweight bout.\n\n[[Bruno Cappelozza]] and [[Rizvan Kuniev]] were scheduled to appear on this card. However, both fighters were suspended by the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]] after failing drug tests. They were removed from the season and replaced by Patrick Brady and Jordan Heiderman.{{Cite web |date=2023-05-12 |title=Nine PFL fighters suspended, scrambling season bout\n|Heavyweight\n|Jordan Heiderman\n|def.\n|Patrick Brady\n|TKO (knee injury)\n|1\n|2:26\n|\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|Isaiah Pinson\n|def.\n|Denzel Freeman\n|Decision (split) (28\u201329, 29\u201328, 29\u201328)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web |title=PFL 5 |url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2023-pfl-5 |website=www.pflmma.com |access-date=16 June 2023}}\n\n== Standings after event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Heavyweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! bout\n|Heavyweight\n|Jamelle Jones\n|def.\n|[[Klidson Abreu]]\n|KO (punches)\n|1\n|1:43\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n\n== Standings After Event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout, with the fighter who missed weight being deducted 1 standings point; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded. A fighter who was unable to compete for any reason, will receive a 1-point penalty (-1 point in the standings). The fighters who made weight will not receive a walkover, but will earn points and contracted purse amounts based on their performance in the altered matchup.{{Cite web|title=2021 Official Rules|url=https://pflmma-prod.s3.amazonaws.com/assets/downloads/2021_Rules_and_Regulations_4-26-2021.pdf}}\n\n=== Heavyweight ===\n{{#section:2021 in Professional Fighters League|HW}}\n\n===Lightweight===\n{{#section:2021 in Professional Fighters League|LW}}\n\n===Women's Lightweight===\n{{#section:2021 in Professional Fighters League|WLW}}\n\n===Featherweight===\n{{#section:2021 in Professional Fighters League|FTW}}\n\u265b = Clinched playoff spot\n\nE = Eliminated\n\n== See also ==\n*[[List of PFL events]]\n*[[List of current PFL fighters]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n\n{{World Series of Fighting Events}}\n{{2021 in mixed martial arts}}\n\n[[Category:Professional Fighters League events]]\n[[Category:2021 in mixed martial arts]]\n[[Category:June 2021 sports events in the United States]]\n[[Category:Organized events in Atlantic City, New Jersey]]"}}}]},"70924276":{"pageid":70924276,"ns":0,"title":"PFL 6 (2022)","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","*":"{{short description|Professional Fighters League MMA event in 2022}}\n\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=PFL 6\n|image=File:PFL 6 (2022 season).jpg\n|image_size=221\n|promotion=[[Professional Fighters League]]\n|date={{start date|2022|07|1}}\n|venue=Overtime Elite Arena\n|city=[[Atlanta, Georgia]]\n|attendance=\n|estimatedviewers=\n|previousevent=[[PFL 5 (2022 season)|PFL 5]]\n|followingevent=[[PFL 7 (2022 season)|PFL 7]]\n}}\n{{Main|PFL (2022 season)}}\n\nThe '''''PFL 6''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2022 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on July 1, 2022, at the Overtime Elite Arena in [[Atlanta, Georgia]]. This marked the sixth regular season event of the tournament and included fights in the Women's [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] and [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] divisions.\n\n== Background ==\nThe event was to be headlined by a women's lightweight clash between the 2019 and 2021 champion [[Kayla Harrison]] will face former [[Bellator Women's Featherweight Champion]] [[Julia Budd]]. However, a week before the event, Budd pulled out due to injury and was replaced by [[Kaitlin Young]].{{Cite web |last=Marrocco |first=Steven |date=2022-06-23 |title=Kayla Harrison now meets Kaitlin Young at PFL 6 after Julia Budd injury |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/6/23/23181048/kayla-harrison-now-meets-kaitlin-young-at-pfl-6-after-julia-budd-injury |access-date=2022-06-24 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}\n\nThe co-main event featured former [[Bellator Welterweight Champion|Bellator Welterweight champion]] [[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] face off against [[Sadibou Sy]]. The rest of the main card saw a welterweight bout between 2019 and 2021 champion [[Ray Cooper III]] against [[Bellator MMA]] and ACA vet [[Brett Cooper (fighter)|Brett Cooper]] while [[Magomed Magomedkerimov]] was scheduled to face off against Jo\u00e3o Zeferino.{{Cite web |date=2022-06-01 |title=Harrison gets toughest test yet vs. Budd at PFL 6 |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/34019101/kayla-harrison-face-tough-challenge-julia-budd-pfl-6 |access-date=2022-06-01 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2022-06-01 |title=Kayla Harrison vs. Julia Budd headlines 2022 PFL 6 on July 1 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/kayla-harrison-vs-julia-budd-pfl-6-headliner-booked |access-date=2022-06-01 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}{{Cite web |last=Holland |first=Jesse |date=2022-06-01 |title=Kayla Harrison, Rory MacDonald join PFL 6 lineup on July 1 in Atlanta |url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/6/1/23150191/kayla-harrison-rory-macdonald-join-pfl-6-lineup-july-1-atlanta-mma |access-date=2022-06-01 |website=MMAmania.com |language=en}} At weigh-ins, Zeferino didn't weigh in and was replaced in his bout against [[Magomed Magomedkerimov]] by Dilano Taylor.{{Cite web |date=2022-06-30 |title=PFL 2022, Week 6 weigh-in results: Kayla Harrison, Rory MacDonald official; Dilano Taylor steps in late |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/06/pfl-2022-week-6-weigh-in-results-kayla-harrison-rory-macdonald-dilano-taylor |access-date=2022-07-01 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Gleison Tibau]] and Nikolai Aleksakhin was scheduled at this event, however Tibau pulled out of the bout and was replaced by Carlos Leal.{{Cite web |title=2022 PFL6 Fight Card Updates |url=https://twitter.com/pflmma/status/1541547558069215232 |access-date=2022-06-27 |website=Twitter |language=en}} After making weight, Aleksakhin withdrew from the bout due to complications. Leal receives a walkover win and 3 points in the welterweight standings.{{Cite web |title=OFFICIAL: Nikolai Aleksakhin has withdrawn from his fight |url=https://twitter.com/pflmma/status/1542553096525684737 |access-date=2022-07-01 |website=Twitter |language=en}}\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN/ESPN+)}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Women's Lightweight|[[Kayla Harrison]]|def.|[[Kaitlin Young]]|TKO (punches)|1|2:35|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|[[Sadibou Sy]]|def.|[[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]]|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 29\u201328)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|[[Ray Cooper III]]|def.|[[Brett Cooper (fighter)|Brett Cooper]]|TKO (knees and punches)|1|0:24|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|[[Magomed Magomedkerimov]]|def.|Dilano Taylor|TKO (punches)|2|3:26|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Women's Lightweight|[[Larissa Pacheco]]|def.|[[Genah Fabian]]|TKO (punches)|1|2:39|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Welterweight|[[Magomed Umalatov]]|def.|Jarrah Al Silawi|KO (punch)|1|3:33|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (ESPN+)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Women's Lightweight|[[Marina Mokhnatkina]]|def.|Abigail Montes|Decision (split) (30\u201327, 27\u201330, 30\u201327)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Women's Lightweight|Martina Jindrov\u00e1|def.|Zamzagul Fayzallanova|TKO (punches)|1|4:04|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Women's Lightweight|Olena Kolesnyk|def.|[[Vanessa Melo]]|Decision (unanimous) (30\u201327, 30\u201327, 30\u201327)|3|5:00|\n}}\n{{MMAevent end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web|url=https://www.pflmma.com/event/2022-pfl-6|title=PFL 6 (2022 season)|website=pflmma.com|access-date=2022-06-27}}\n\n== Standings After Event ==\nThe PFL points system is based on results of the match. The winner of a fight receives 3 points. If the fight ends in a draw, both fighters will receive 1 point. The bonus for winning a fight in the first, second, or third round is 3 points, 2 points, and 1 point respectively. The bonus for winning in the third round requires a fight be stopped before 4:59 of the third round. No bonus point will be awarded if a fighter wins via decision. For example, if a fighter wins a fight in the first round, then the fighter will receive 6 total points. A decision win will result in three total points. If a fighter misses weight, the opponent (should they comply with weight limits) will receive 3 points due to a walkover victory, regardless of winning or losing the bout; if the non-offending fighter subsequently wins with a stoppage, all bonus points will be awarded.{{cite web |title=Rules and Regulations |url=http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |url-status=dead |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20180808170826/http://www.pflmma.com/rules-regulations/ |archive-date=2018-08-08 |access-date=2019-04-28 |publisher=PFL}}\n\n===Welterweight===\n{| class=\"wikitable sortable\"\n! 8]]\n}}\n\n{{Main|PFL (2021 season)}}\nThe '''''PFL 7''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2021 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on August 13, 2021. This was the start of the playoffs for the [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] and [[lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] divisions. This event aired on [[ESPN2]], with the prelims on [[ESPN+]].{{Cite web|last=Lee|first=Alexander K.|date=2021-06-08|title=PFL 2021 playoff dates announced for August|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/8/22524479/pfl-2021-playoff-dates-announced-for-august|access-date=2021-06-26|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\n==Background==\n[[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] and [[Ray Cooper III]] headlined the first PFL playoff card, with the [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] and [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] playoffs holding their semifinal bouts. MacDonald made his third appearance with PFL cage, going 1-1 in the regular season, with his most recent outing being a highly controversial decision loss to [[Gleison Tibau]]. He took on the former PFL champion Cooper, who was 2-0 during the regular season in 2021.{{Cite web|date=2021-07-27|title=2021 PFL Playoffs 1 additions include Oliver Aubin-Mercier vs. Natan Schulte|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/2021-pfl-playoffs-1-additions-include-oliver-aubin-mercier-vs-natan-schulte|access-date=2021-07-28|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}}\n\nThe Lightweight bracket saw UFC and boxing veteran [[Clay Collard]] take on [[Raush Manfio]] while Loik Radzhabov and Alexander Martinez fought in the other lightweight semifinal.{{Cite web|last=Newswire|first=MMA Fighting|date=2021-07-27|title=PFL announces full card for first playoff event headlined by Rory MacDonald vs. Ray Cooper III on Aug. 13|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/27/22596391/pfl-announces-full-card-for-first-playoff-event-headlined-by-rory-macdonald-vs-ray-cooper-3-aug-13|access-date=2021-07-28|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\nFeatured additions included a lightweight contest include two-time PFL champion Natan Schulte taking on 12-time UFC veteran [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]], as well as planned bout with Swedish PFL vet [[Sadibou Sy]] facing 28-time UFC veteran [[Gleison Tibau]].\n\nOn August 4, it was announced that Jo\u00e3o Zeferino was out of his Semi-Final match-up against [[Magomed Magomedkerimov]], with #5 ranked [[Sadibou Sy]] taking his place. [[Curtis Millender]] (replacing Sy) was then scheduled to face [[Gleison Tibau]]. Natan Schulte was also announced to have been scratched, with [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] now fighting [[Darrell Horcher]]. Lastly, Nikolay Aleksakhin was removed from his bout against [[Brett Cooper (fighter)|Brett Cooper]], with Cooper now facing Tyler Hill.{{Cite web|date=2021-08-04|title=2021 PFL Playoffs 1 sees four matchups altered, one fight added|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/pfl-playoffs-1-undergoes-changes-natan-schulte-out|access-date=2021-08-04|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}} On August 12, Millender was announced to have pulled out of the bout against Tibau, so Tibau instead faced Micah Terrill, while [[Magomed Umalatov]] faced [[Leandro Silva (fighter)|Leandro Silva]].{{Cite web|title=Updated Fight Card for Tomorrow Night's Prelims!|url=https://twitter.com/pflmma/status/1425824170789638152|access-date=2021-08-12|website=Twitter|language=en}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, [[Darrell Horcher]] and [[Leandro Silva (fighter)|Leandro Silva]] weighed in at 159.25 and 172.5 pounds, missing weight by 3.25 and 1.5 pounds respectively. The bouts proceeded at catchweight and they were fined 20% of their purses, which went to their opponents [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] and [[Magomed Umalatov]] respectively.{{Cite [[Hulu Theater]] in [[New York City, New York]], United States. This event marked the start of the playoffs for the [[Light heavyweight (MMA)|Light Heavyweight]] and [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |title=2022 PFL playoff schedule includes U.K. doubleheader, promotion\u2019s first international shows |url=https://ca.sports.yahoo.com/news/2022-pfl-playoff-schedule-includes-130943888.html |access-date=2022-07-02 |website=ca.sports.yahoo.com |language=en-CA}}{{Cite web |last=Lee |first=Alexander K. |date=2022-05-24 |title=PFL to make London debut for 2022 playoffs, postseason begins in New York on Aug. 5 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/5/24/23139419/pfl-to-make-london-debut-for-2022-playoffs-postseason-begins-in-new-york-on-aug-5 |access-date=2022-07-02 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2022-05-24 |title=2022 PFL playoff schedule includes U.K. doubleheader, promotion's first international shows |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/2022-pfl-playoffs-international-uk-new-york |access-date=2022-07-02 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\nThis event featured the semifinal bouts of the [[Light heavyweight (MMA)|Light Heavyweight]] and [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] playoffs. \n\nRob Wilkinson and Delan Monte will face each other in the Light Heavyweight division. Wilkinson made his third appearance with PFL cage, going 2-0 in the regular season with a first and second round stoppages. Monte was 1-1 during the regular season in 2022. The other side of the bracket was to see two former UFC veterans, [[Ant\u00f4nio Carlos J\u00fanior]] and [[Omari Akhmedov]], face off against each other. However, Antonio suffered an ACL injury requiring surgery, sidelining him for the rest of the year and he was replaced by Josh Silveira for the playoffs.{{Cite web |last=Cruz |first=Guilherme |date=2022-07-08 |title=Defending PFL champion Antonio Carlos Junior injured, out of 2022 playoffs Lightweight bracket saw former UFC Champion [[Anthony Pettis]] take on UFC vet [[Stevie Ray (fighter)|Stevie Ray]] in a rematch of their bout six weeks earlier, while [[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]] and Alex Martinez faced off in the other lightweight semifinal.\n\n== Results ==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (ESPN)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Stevie Ray (fighter)|Stevie As a result, Alencar's original opponent of [[Mike Kyle]] was paired with Mo De'Reese in a bout to determine a new alternate.\n\n[[Timur Valiev]] was expected to face Alexandre de Almeida in a playoff bout at this event, however, Valiev was removed due to injury and replaced by the highest ranked alternate on the undercard, [[Jumabieke Tuerxun]]. As a result, Tuerxun's original opponent of [[Marcos Galv\u00e3o]] was set to face [[Jeremy Kennedy]] in a bout to determine a new alternate. However, on the day of the event Kennedy dropped out due to undisclosed reasons, and so Galvao was automatically pushed forward as the new alternate instead.\n\n==Fight Fighters League mixed martial arts event in 2021}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=PFL 8\n|image=ProfessionalFighterLeague82021.jpg\n|promotion=[[Professional Fighters League]]\n|date= {{start date|2021|08|19}}\n|venue= [[Seminole Hard Rock Hotel & Casino Hollywood]]\n|city= [[Hollywood, Florida]], United States\n|attendance= \n|estimatedviewers = \n|previousevent=[[PFL 7 (2021 season)|PFL 7]]\n|followingevent=[[PFL 9 (2021 season)|PFL 9]]\n}}\n\n{{Main|PFL (2021 season)}}\nThe '''''PFL 8''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2021 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on August 19, 2021. This was the second event for of the playoffs with the [[Heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] and Women's [[Lightweight (MMA)|Lightweight]] divisions competing. This event aired on [[ESPN]], with the prelims on [[ESPN+]].{{Cite web|last=Lee|first=Alexander K.|date=2021-06-08|title=PFL 2021 playoff dates announced for August|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/8/22524479/pfl-2021-playoff-dates-announced-for-august|access-date=2021-06-26|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\n==Background==\n[[Kayla Harrison]], the only two-time Olympic gold medalist in judo in the history of the United States, entered her second PFL playoffs unbeaten in 10 pro bouts. She faced [[Genah Fabian]] who held a 4-1 record and had won both of her PFL regular season bouts against Julija Pajic and Laura Sanchez to advance to the playoffs. This is the second time that Harrison and Fabian had been scheduled to fight in the PFL. They were set to meet in the 2019 semifinals in October of that year, but Fabian withdrew due to an illness after issues with her weight cut.{{Cite web|last=Newswire|first=MMA Fighting|date=2021-08-03|title=PFL announces full card for second playoff event headlined by Kayla Harrison vs. Genah Fabian on Aug. 19|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/3/22607421/pfl-announces-full-card-second-playoff-event-headlined-by-kayla-harrison-vs-genah-fabian-on-aug-19|access-date=2021-08-04|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\nThe other lightweight semifinal was expected to feature 2019 finalist [[Larissa Pacheco]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships|Invicta FC]] fighter Taylor Guardado.{{Cite web|date=2021-08-03|title=Kayla Harrison vs. Genah Fabian headlines 2021 PFL Playoffs 2|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/kayla-harrison-vs-genah-fabian-2021-pfl-playoffs-2-lineup-mma-matchup-fight-booking|access-date=2021-08-04|website=MMA Junkie|language=en-US}} However, at the weigh-ins, [[Larissa Pacheco]] missed weight by 2 pounds and therefore was removed from the tournament and was replaced by 5th seeded [[Mariana Morais]]. Morais's original opponent, Claudia Zamora, instead faced [[Marina Mokhnatkina]] after [[Kaitlin Young]], Marina's opponent, was removed from the card.{{Cite web|last=Lee|first=Alexander K.|date=2021-08-18|title=Larissa Pacheco out of PFL playoffs after missing weight, Mariana Morais to fight Taylor Guardado at PFL 8|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/18/22631285/larissa-pacheco-out-of-pfl-playoffs-after-missing-weight-mariana-morais-fight-taylor-guardado-pfl-8|access-date=2021-08-19|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\nThe heavyweight playoffs also began. Top seed [[Bruno Cappelozza]] took on Jamelle Jones, who qualified for the playoffs with a knockout of [[Klidson Abreu]] after stepping in as an alternate. [[Denis Goltsov]] and Ante Delija also met in the other heavyweight semifinal.{{Cite web|last=Sherdog.com|title=Professional Fighters League Adds Two European Prospects to Women\u2019s Lightweight for 2022|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Professional-Fighters-League-Adds-Two-European-Prospects-to-Womens-Lightweight-for-2022-180761|access-date=2021-08-04|website=Sherdog}}{{Cite '''''PFL 8''''' [[mixed martial arts]] event for the 2022 season of the [[Professional Fighters League]] was held on August 13, 2022, at the [[Cardiff Motorpoint Arena]] in [[Cardiff]], Wales. This was the start of the playoffs for the [[Heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] and [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] divisions.{{Cite web |title=2022 PFL playoff schedule includes U.K. doubleheader, promotion\u2019s first international shows |url=https://ca.sports.yahoo.com/news/2022-pfl-playoff-schedule-includes-130943888.html |access-date=2022-07-02 |website=ca.sports.yahoo.com |language=en-CA}}{{Cite web |last=Lee |first=Alexander K. |date=2022-05-24 |title=PFL to make London debut for 2022 playoffs, postseason begins in New York on Aug. 5 |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/5/24/23139419/pfl-to-make-london-debut-for-2022-playoffs-postseason-begins-in-new-york-on-aug-5 |access-date=2022-07-02 |website=MMA Fighting |language=en}}{{Cite web |date=2022-05-24 |title=2022 PFL playoff schedule includes U.K. doubleheader, promotion's first international shows |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/2022-pfl-playoffs-international-uk-new-york |access-date=2022-07-02 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\n== Background ==\nThis will mark the second PFL playoff card, along with being the first PFL event held outside of America. The event will mark the start of the [[Heavyweight (MMA)|Heavyweight]] and [[Welterweight (MMA)|Welterweight]] playoffs, with UFC veteran and former Bellator champion [[Rory MacDonald (fighter)|Rory MacDonald]] scheduled to take on undefeated [[Magomed Umalatov]]. On the other side of the bracket, [[Sadibou Sy]] takes on Carlos Leal.{{Cite web |date=2022-05-24 |title=2022 PFL playoff schedule includes U.K. doubleheader, promotion's first international shows |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/05/2022-pfl-playoffs-international-uk-new-york |access-date=2022-07-02 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}} The Heavyweight bracket was to see Denis Goltsov taking on 2021 PFL Champion Bruno Cappelozza, while Ante Delija faced [[Renan Ferreira (fighter)|Renan Ferreira]]. However after picking up an injury, Cappelozza was unable to continue in the tournament and replaced by Matheus Scheffel.{{Cite web |last=Raimondi |first=Marc |date=2022-07-28 |title=Sources: Injured Cappelozza out of PFL playoffs |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/34307450/sources-defending-pfl-heavyweight-champ-bruno-cappelozza-playoffs-undisclosed-injury |access-date=2022-07-30 |website=ESPN.com |language=en}} After visa issues forced [[Magomed Umalatov|Umalatov]] and Goltsov to pull out of their respective bouts due to visa issues, they were replaced bu Dilano Taylor and This was the third event of the playoffs with the [[Featherweight (MMA)|Featherweight]] and [[Light heavyweight (MMA)|Light Heavyweight]] divisions competing. This event aired on [[ESPN2]], with the prelims airing on [[ESPN+]].{{Cite web|last=Lee|first=Alexander K.|date=2021-06-08|title=PFL 2021 playoff dates announced for August|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/8/22524479/pfl-2021-playoff-dates-announced-for-august|access-date=2021-06-26|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\n==Background==\nThe fight card marked the final playoff event with featherweights and light heavyweights competing in the playoff semifinals.\n\nHeadliners [[Brendan Loughnane]] and [[Movlid Khaybulaev]] met in a featherweight contest, each fighter looking for their first PFL Championship berth. Loughnane debuted in Season 2 with a pair of wins in one-off fights, while Khaybulaev fell short in the Season 2 quarterfinals after suffering a knockout loss against Daniel Pineda, who was later suspended for an anti-doping violation.{{Cite web|last=Newswire|first=MMA Fighting|date=2021-08-10|title=PFL announces full 2021 Playoff 3 card, Brendan Loughnane vs. Movlid Khaybulaev headlines|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/10/22619185/pfl-announces-full-2021-playoff-3-card-brendan-loughnane-vs-movlid-khaybulaev-headlines|access-date=2021-08-24|website=MMA Fighting|language=en}}\n\nThe other featherweight bracket saw the wrestling standout [[Bubba Jenkins]], in his first PFL season, take on three-time PFL Playoff competitor [[Chris Wade (fighter)|Chris Wade]] and light heavyweights [[Cezar Ferreira]] and Marthin Hamlet met for a shot at fighting in the PFL Championship. The other light heavyweight bout saw Season 2 light heavyweight champ and No. 4 seed [[Emiliano Sordi]] take on UFC veteran [[Ant\u00f4nio Carlos J\u00fanior]].{{Cite web|title=PROFESSIONAL FIGHTERS LEAGUE ANNOUNCES FULL PLAYOFF 3 CARD SET FOR FRIDAY, AUGUST 27 ON ESPN2|url=https://www.pflmma.com/news/professional-fighters-league-announces-full-playoff-3-card-set-for-friday-august-27-on-espn2|access-date=2021-08-24|website=www.pflmma.com}}\n\nAt weigh-ins, [[Bobby Moffett]] weighed in at 148.2 pounds, missing weight by 2.2 pounds. The bout proceeded at catchweight and Moffett was fined a percentage of his purse, which went to [[Jason Knight (fighter)|Jason Knight]].{{Cite web|last=Sherdog.com|title=PFL 9: 2021 Playoffs Weigh-in Results: Featherweight & Light Heavyweight Semifinals
